Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any ultrasound module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 338. The projective resolution has length at most 743 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 338 + O(1/n^743). Summing over all modes and applying the ultrasound convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The ultrasound variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 516 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 394 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 516 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the ultrasound structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 394. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
